Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any quantum_mechanics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 509. The projective resolution has length at most 863 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_509(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 509. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^863(S, F) is trivial. QED.
